avirtaḷir puriyum mēṉiyar avirtoṟum
poṉṉuri kaṭukkun titaliyar iṉṉakip
parumantāṅkiya paṇintēn talkul
mācil makaḷiroṭu maṟuviṉṟi viḷaṅka
(tirumuruku 138_147)
eṉavarum toṭarkaḷāl caṅkap pulavarākiya nakkīrar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṅṅaṉamē tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ kālattum teṉ ṉāṭṭut tirukkōyilkaḷil iṟivaṉic cēvittut teyvappāṭal pāṭum makaḷir, iṉiya ici moḻiyum uṭal vaṉappum tūymiyum naṟkuṇaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟukkum iṭaṉākiya uḷḷattūymiyum oruṅkuṭiyarāy, ellām valla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷatu aruḷmēṉi vaṇṇaṅkaḷum varampilāṟṟalum nalamvaḷar ñāṉurikaḷum ākiya peruñcīrttikaḷip poruḷākak koṇṭa iṉṉicic ceḻum pāṭalkaḷi iṟivaṉ tirumuṉṉarp pāṭip pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. icceytiyiṉi,
		paṇṇiṉalla moḻiyār pavaḷattuvar vāyiṉār
eṇṇiṉalla kuṇattār iṇivēlveṉṟa kaṇṇiṉār
vaṇṇampāṭi valipāṭittam vāymoḻi pāṭavē
aṇṇal kēṭṭuntāṉum aiyāṟuṭi aiyaṉē
eṉavarum tiruppāṭalil āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār aḻakuṟak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
paṇṇarnta pāṭalkaḷāl iṟivaṉip pāṭippōṟṟu talākiya ittiruppaṇi yāvarālum virumpi mēṟkoḷḷat takka eḷimiyum iṉimiyum oruṅkuṭiyateṉpatum, ivviṉiya cātaṉatti mēṟkoḷḷa maṉamillātār itaṉiṉ mikka maṟṟit toṇṭukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu patti ceyya iyalāteṉpatum aruḷanupavamuṭiya aṭiyārkaḷiṉ tuṇipākum.
		pattaṉāyp pāṭamāṭṭēṉ paramaṉē paramayōkī
ettiṉāṟ patticeykēṉ eṉṉinī yikaḻavēṇṭā
eṉa apparaṭikaḷ tillic ciṟṟampalavaṉi yiṟiñcikkūṟum ivvēṇṭukōḷ, iṟivaṉpāṟ patti ceytaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta eḷiya cātaṉam pāṭumpaṇiyē eṉṟa ivvuṇmiyiṉi naṉku valiyuṟuttal kāṇalām.
tirukkōyilkaḷil tiruvalakiṭutal, meḻukutal naṟumalar māli toṭuttal eṉṟa iccariyic toṇṭukaḷ oṉṟiṉukku oṉṟu pattumaṭaṅku mēṟpaṭṭa naṟpayaṉkaḷit taruvaṉa. tirukkōyilil tiruviḷakkiṭuvōr uṇmi neṟiyākiya civañāṉattip peṟuvar. iṟivaṉatu meyp pukaḻi viritturikkum iṉṉicip pāṭalākiya kītatti mikappāṭum aṭiyārkaḷukku iṟivaṉ aruḷuntiṟam, mēṟkuṟitta toṇṭukaḷāl viḷiyum naṟpayaṉkaḷik kāṭṭilum aḷavaṟṟaṉavākum. ittakiya tiruttoṇṭiṉ payaṉkaḷi aṟivuṟuttum muṟiyil amintatu,
viḷakkiṉār peṟṟiṉpam meḻukkiṉāṟ patiṟṟiyākum
tuḷakkil naṉmalar toṭuttāl tūyaviṇṇēṟa lākum
viḷakkiṭṭār pēṟucolliṉ meynneṟi ñāṉamākum
aḷappila kītañcoṉṉārkku aṭikaḷtām aruḷumāṟē
eṉavarum tirunēriciyākum. itaṉ īṟṟaṭiyiṉi
kītañcoṉṉārkku aṭikaḷ aruḷumāṟu aḷappila
eṉa iyittup poruḷ koḷka. (tām-aci) iṉi, ittiruppāṭalil, tiruvalakiṭutal mutaliya paṇikaḷic ceytār peṟum pēṟu ivvaḷavu ivvaḷavu eṉa uṇarttiya aṭikaḷ, pukaḻttiṟattāl aḷavillaṉavākiya icippāṭal kaḷāl iṟivaṉip pōṟṟiṉārkku avaṟṟiṉum mēlākak tarattakka perumiyuṭiya poruḷ piṟitiṉmiyiṉ, paramaṉiyē pāṭuvārākiya avvaṭiyār kaḷukku iṟivaṉ taṉṉittāṉē pariciṟ poruḷāka vaḻaṅkiyaruḷvāṉ eṉpār, aṉappila kītañcoṉṉārkku aruḷumāṟu (kimmāṟāka nalkattakka poruḷ) aṭikaḷ tām (iṟivaṉē) eṉṟār eṉak koḷḷutaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu- ittiruppāṭal, iṟivaṉi aṉpiṉāl vaḻi paṭutaṟku iṉiya cātaṉamāka viḷaṅkuvaṉa icip pāṭalkaḷēyeṉṟa uṇmiyiḷi naṉku valiyuṟuttal kāṇka.
taṉakkuvami yillātāṉākiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu uytipeṟutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta cātaṉamāka aminta iciyāṉatu. ivvulaka vāḻkkiyil makkaṭ kulattār tammuṭ pakiyiṉṟi aṉpuṭiyarāy maṉaṅkalantu paḻakutaṟkum kāma vekuḷi mayakkam eṉappaṭum maṉamācu nīṅki uyarnta poruḷi eṇṇutaṟkum eṉṟum iṉpam perukum iyalpiṉāl naṉṟē purintu evvuyirkkum iṉiyarāy iṉitu vāḻtaṟkum iṉiya uyirttuṉiyākavum tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
ellōrum kaṇṭu añcutaṟkuriya nañcuṭiya pām piṉiyum pākaṉukkaṭaṅkāta matayāṉiyiṉiyum āṟali kaḷvar mutaliya koṭiyōrkaḷiyum koṭumikaḷintu aṭaṅki yoḻukac ceyyum āṟṟal vāyntatu ici eṉpataṉip pulavarkaḷ tam kāppiyaṅkaḷila naṉku viḷakki yuḷḷārkaḷ.
cevippulaṉukku iṉpam tarutalum, tuṉpattāṟ kalakkamuṟṟa uḷḷattiṟkut tēṟutalaḷittut teḷiviṉi nalkutalum, aimpoṟikaḷāl īrkkappaṭṭup puṟattē yaliyum maṉattiṉit tīmiyiṉ nīkki naṉṟiṉpāl niṟuttu talu, makkaḷiṉ maṉaviruḷ nīṅka avartam maṉattakattē aṟivoḷiyit tōṟṟuvittalum, makkaḷ paḻinīkkit tiruntutaṟkēṟṟa vaṉcollāṟ kuṟṟaṅkaṭitalum, ceyyat takkatu itu eṉpataṉi aiyuṟavukku iṭamiṉṟi iṟutiyāka muṭittuk kūṟutalum, ciṉattāl mikiceyya eḻuntāri vekuḷiyit taṇittu meṉmippaṭuttutalum, maṉat tuyarāl taḷarcciyuṟṟāric cōrvu nīkkik kiḷarn teḻumpaṭi ūkkamaḷittu uyarttutalum ākiya ivi eṭṭum icip pāṭalkaḷiṉ eṇvakip payaṉkaḷākum.
		iṉpan teḷivē niṟiyō ṭoḷiyē
vaṉcol iṟuti mantam uccameṉa
vanta eṭṭum pāṭaliṉ payaṉē
	(cilap. araṅkēṟṟu-urimēṟkōḷ)
eṉavarum nūṟpā, icippāṭalkaḷāl ulaka makkaḷ eytutaṟkuriya immip payaṉkaḷ iṉpam, teḷivu, niṟi, oḷi, vaṉcol, iṟuti, mantam, uccam eṉa eṭṭākum eṉat tokutturittal kāṇalām. cevviyamuṟiyil vācikkappeṟum nalliciyāṉatu makkaḷuḷḷattē paṭin tuḷḷa maṉamācukaḷik kaḻuvit tūyanaṉṉeñcattiṉarāy evvuyirkkum aṉpuṭiyarāy vāḻum iṉpa vāḻkkiyiṉi nalkum eṉpatu,
makara vīṇiyiṉ maṉamācu kaḻīi
	(peruṅ. uñci. ṅaru 218)
eṉavarum koṅkuvēḷ vāymoḻiyāl iṉitu pulaṉākum.


8. tamiḻici iyakkam
tamiḻnāṭu tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭa kaṭic caṅkakālam variyil, icittamiḻ naṉku vaḷarntu vantatu. piṉpu, ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭip pakuti mutal ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭuvariyil tamiḻnāṭu ayalār āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭiruntamiyiṉāl, akkālattil icit tamiḻ pōṟṟuvāraṟṟu vīḻcciyaṭintatu. ki. pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉpavaṉ kaḷappirarip pōril veṉṟu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. akkālam mutal, annāṭu pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ āṭcikku uḷḷāyiṟṟu. annāṭṭiṉ talinakaramākiya maturiyil, ōr icic caṅkam niṟuvi, icittamiḻip pāṇṭiya maṉṉar vaḷarttaṉar.
		kūṭaliṉ āynta oṇṭīn tamiḻiṉ
tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō aṉṟi
ēḻicic cūḻal pukkō	(tirukkōvi)
eṉṉum māṇikkavācakar vāymoḻi itaṉip pulap paṭuttum. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉoruvaṉ, taṉ ariyaṇikku iciyaḷavu kaṇṭāṉ eṉap peyariṭṭiruntaṉaṉ eṉpatum iṅkē kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil tiruñāṉa campantarum tirunāvukkaracarum tamiḻōṭu icipāṭi icittamiḻi vaḷarttaṉar. vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉarum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vantu icipayiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa uṇmi, tiruñāṉacampantar tēvārattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. akkālattil toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉiyum cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiyum āṭcipurinta pallava vēntaṉākiya mutal makēntiravarmaṉ icik kaliyil vallavaṉāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. kuṭumiyāṉ maliyil avaṉ varinta icikkalveṭṭiṉāl avaṉatu icip pulami iṉitu pulaṉām, ki. pi. 8, 9-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉārum periyāḻvār mutaliya āḻvārkaḷum aruṭ pākkaḷ pāṭi, iciyi vaḷarttaṉar. ki. pi. oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil mutal ātitta cōḻaṉ cōḻar pēraraci niṟuviṉāṉ. ivaṉāl niṟuvappeṟṟa cōḻappēraracu ki. pi. patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti variyil (nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaṭku mēl) naṭipeṟṟatu. akkālattil cōḻa aracarkaḷ, civa camaya kuravarkaḷiṉ tiruppatikaṅkaḷit tirukkōyilkaḷil nāḷtōṟum paṇkaḷuṭaṉ pāṭuvataṟku nivantaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅki icit tamiḻi vaḷarttaṉar. mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ, icittamiḻ nūloṉṟu iyaṟṟi yiruntāṉ eṉṟu kaliṅkattupparaṇi kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. accōḻaṉiṉ maṉivi icippulamiyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉamiyāl ēḻici vallapi eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟṟāḷ.
ivvāṟu iṉitu vaḷarntu mika uyarniliyilirunta icittamiḻ piṟkālattil ayalavar paṭiyeḻucciyāl uṇṭākiya kuḻappattiṉāl vīḻcciyaṭintatu. piṉ vicayanakara vēntar paṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻakattiṉit taṉṉaṭip paṭuttiṉamiyālum, ataṉpiṉ aracāṇṭa nāyakka maṉṉar kaḷiṉ viruppattiṟku ēṟpavum teluṅku icippāṭalkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ paravalāyiṉa. innili ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari māṟāmaliruntatu. teluṅka maṉṉariṉ āṭci nīṅkiyum teluṅkup pāṭalkaḷiṉ āṭci nīṅkavilli. itaṉāl, tamiḻakattil nikaḻntuvanta iciyaraṅkukaḷ palavaṟṟilum piṟamoḻip pāṭalkaḷē mikutiyākap pāṭappaṭṭaṉa araṅkiṉ iṟutiyil tukkaṭā eṉṟa peyaril iraṇṭoru tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷē pāṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
inniliyila, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṟpēṟākat tōṉṟi aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakam niṟuvit tamiḻt tāykku ākkamum ūkkamum aḷitta peruṅkoṭivaḷḷalākiya ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracar ṭākṭar rājā car aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷ icittamiḻukku ākkamaḷikka muṉ vantārkaḷ.
poruḷ teriyāta vēṟṟumoḻip pāṭalkaḷāl tamiḻar poruḷuṇarcciyuṭaṉ iciyiṉpattiṉi nukara iyalavilliyē eṉa eṇṇiya vaḷḷal aṇṇāmali yaracaravarkaḷ, caṅkakālattilum piṟkālattilum tamiḻakattil ciṟappāka vaḷarntu vanta tamiḻicikku uriya iṭattiṉit tarutal vēṇṭumeṉa uḷaṅkiḷarntu eḻuntārkaḷ. namnāṭṭu iciyaraṅkukaḷil vēṟṟumoḻip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭap peṟuvatu niyāyamilli; payaṉumilli eṉṟa uṇarcci aṇṇāmaliyaracar avarkaḷ uḷḷattil āḻamāka vērūṉṟiyatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil  pāṭappeṟukiṉṟa icip pāṭalkaḷ tamiḻppāṭalkaḷākavē iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ peruvēṭkiyiṉi niṟivēṟṟum poṟuppu aṇṇāmali vaḷḷaliṉ vāḻkkik kuṟik kōḷāka malarvatāyiṟṟu. atuvē tamiḻiciyiyakkamāka uruveṭuttatu. tamiḻici eṉṉum payir taḻittu vaḷara aṇṇāmali vaḷḷaleṉṉum mukil poruḷmaḻiyip peytatu.
tamiḻiciyiyakkam vaḷarntu vanta kālattil aṇṇā mali aracaravarkaḷiṉ aṟupatāmāṇṭu niṟivu viḻāvum vantatu. aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakam ivarkaḷiṉ aṟupatām āṇṭu niṟivu viḻāviṉi 30-9-1941 il mikacciṟappākak koṇṭāṭiyatu. appōtu ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracaravarkaḷ, tamiḻil icippāṭalkaḷ iyaṟṟuvati ūkkuvittaṟporuṭṭum, putuppāṭalkaḷip payiṟṟu vittup pāṭummuṟi nāṭṭil paravutaṟporuṭṭum patiṉiyāyiram rūpāviṉi maṇiviḻā naṉkoṭiyāka aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattiṟku aḷittārkaḷ. tamiḻicik kaḻakaṅkaṭkup poruḷutavi ceytal, tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷē pāṭappeṟum iciyaraṅkukaḷi amittal, putiya tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ iyaṟṟutal, ariyapaḻiya tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷik kaṟpikkum ciṟanta icik kaliñarip pārāṭṭi naṉkoṭiyaḷittal, palkalikkaḻakattāriṉ mēṟpārviyil tokuttu veḷiyiṭappaṭum tamiḻicip pāṭalkaḷik kaṟpittaṟkup paḷḷikaḷ niṟuvi naṭattutal, nāṭṭil ellā iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum ariya tamiḻicip pāṭalkaḷit tiraṭṭutal, tamiḻicip pāṭanūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭutal mutaliya tamiḻici vaḷarccit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.
aracar aṇṇāmali vaḷḷalavarkaḷiṉ viruppat tiṟkum tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ātaraviṟkum ēṟpa, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam, tamiḻici vaḷarccikkut taṉ paṇikaḷi virintu ceyyat toṭaṅkiyatu. tamiḻici paṟṟiya irupatu tokutikaḷ palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭākac cila āṇṭu kaḷukkuḷ veḷivantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṉṉum cila icit tamiḻ nūlkaḷi aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷatu. innūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum cērttup pārkkumpōtu, 1300kku mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻicip pāṭalkaḷ 230 vari uḷḷa irākaṅkaḷil amintuḷḷami kāṇalām. ciṟanta icivāṇkaḷ, ippāṭalkaḷukku iciyamit tuḷḷaṉar. mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa tokutikaḷil aṭaṅkiya ici iyal, tamiḻar icikkaruvikaḷ, curamēḷa kalāniti, mattaḷam kaṟkum muṟiyum ataṉ ciṟappu vakiyum, parata caṅkirakam eṉṟa nūlkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭu ici ilakkaṇa amitikaḷit teḷivāka viḷakkattakkaṉa. mirutaṅkam payilum māṇavar tiṟamipeṟum vaṇṇam ippalkalikkaḻakam veḷippaṭuttiya mirutaṅka pāṭamuṟi eṉṉum nūlum ittokutikaḷil ōṉṟākum. tamiḻicikku arumperum toṇṭukaḷ purinta aṇṇāmali vaḷḷal avarkaḷip pārāṭṭiyum vāḻttiyum pala camayaṅkaḷil takka pulavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ, ciṟanta ici vāṇarkaḷāl ici yamikkap peṟṟuc, ;ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracar icimāli eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka veḷḷi viḻāviṉpōtu veḷivantatu.
iciyil tērccipeṟṟiruntum mikutiyāṉa tamiḻicip pāṭalkaḷik kaṟṟuk koḷḷātavarkaḷ avaṟṟik kaṟṟukkoḷvataṟku utaviyāka, ōrāṇṭut tamiḻici vakuppoṉṟum aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattil toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. ivvakuppilum, nāṉku āṇṭukaḷiṟ kaṟpikkappeṟum caṅkītapūṣaṇa vakuppilum tamiḻicip pāṭalkaḷik kaṟṟut tērnta intiyanāṭṭu māṇavarkaḷum, ilaṅki māṇavarkaḷum paṟpalarāvar. ivvāṟu tamiḻici vaḷarccikku aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakam āṟṟivarum arumpaṇiyil palkalikkaḻaka iṇivēntar ṭākṭar rājācar muttiya ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷ tam tantiyāravar kaḷip pōlavē pērūkkam kāṭṭi ātaravaḷittu varuvatu tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṟpēṟākum.
tamiḻiciyic ciṟappāka vaḷarttaṟ poruṭṭum iciyaraṅkukaḷil tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷiyē mikutiyākap pāṭuntiṟattil icivāṇarkaḷi ūkkuvittaṟ poruṭṭum tamiḻc celvarkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ āṭciyāḷarkaḷ, potu makkaḷ ākiya aṉivaruṭiya ātaravum koṇṭu aṇṇā maliyaracaravarkaḷ 1943 ām āṇṭu mē mātattil tamiḻicic caṅkattiṉit tamiḻakat talinakarākiya ceṉṉiyil niṟuviṉārkaḷ.
tamiḻiciyiyakkam kaṇṭa peruṅkoṭi vaḷḷal rājācar aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷāl muppattāṟu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ tamiḻakattil talinakaramākiya ceṉṉimā nakaril niṟuvappeṟṟa tamiḻicic caṅkam, āṇṭu tōṟum icip perum pulavarkaḷik kūṭṭi iciyaraṅku kaḷit toṭarntu nikaḻtti varuvataṉi yāvarum aṟivar. iccaṅkattiṉ niliyamākat tikaḻum aṇṇāmali maṉṟattil tamiḻicik kallūri toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻicic caṅkattiṉ vaḷarccikku aracaravar kaḷōṭu ōttuḻitta tamiḻppēraṟiñarkaḷ palarāvar.
ceṉṉit tamiḻicic caṅka āṇṭu viḻāvil tēvārap paṇṇārāyccikkeṉa mutal nāṉku nāṭkaḷ variyaṟukkap peṟṟup paṇṇārāyccik kūṭṭam āṇṭu tōṟum toṭarntunikaḻntuvarukiṉṟatu. paṇṇārāyccik kūṭṭat tiṟku iciyulakil pukaḻpeṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷ talimi tāṅkuvatum avarkaḷukku icippēraṟiñar eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappippatum iccaṅkattiṉ marapāka amintuḷḷami palarum aṟinta ceytiyākum.
tēvārap paṇṇārāyccik kūṭṭattil ici marapaṟinta tēvāra ōtuvārkaḷum, karunāṭaka caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷum, iyaṟṟamiḻp pulavarkaḷum, iyalici aṟiñar palarum, centamiḻp pēraṉparkaḷum peruviruppuṭaṉ kalantu koṇṭu taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ ārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭa karuttukkaḷit tiṟampaṭa eṭutturittup paṇṇārāyccikkut tuṇipurintu varuvatu tamiḻ makkaḷāl pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya nallicip paṇiyākum.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tantiyum peruṅ koṭi vaḷḷalumākiya ceṭṭināṭṭaracar aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷip pōlavē avartam tirumakaṉārum aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka iṇi vēntarumākiya ṭākṭar rājācar mu. a. muttiyā ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷ ceṉṉit tamiḻicic caṅkattic ciṟappākap pōṟṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. tamiḻ vaḷarnta maturi mānakarattilum aintāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ tamiḻicic caṅkattiṉit tōṟṟu vittu muttiyā maṉṟam amittu nāḷum iṉṉiciyāl tamiḻ vaḷara vaḻiyamittuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ceṉṉiyilum maturiyilum amintuḷḷa tamiḻicic caṅkaṅkaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻar nuṇṇitiṟ kaṇṭuṇarttiya paṇṇum tiṟamumākiya icinalaṅkaḷ ulakelām parava nallicip paṇipurintu nīṭuniṉṟu palakiḷikaḷuṭaṉ taḻittu vaḷarkaveṉa ēḻiciyāy icippayaṉāy viḷaṅkum iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi kaḷi iṟiñcip pōṟṟutal namatu kaṭamiyākum.


9. icit tamiḻ vaḷarccikku
ākkamākum paṇikaḷ
āyiram yāṇṭukaḷāka maṟintukiṭanta paḻantamiḻ icic celvattit tamiḻ makkaḷ mīṭṭum peṟutaṟku utavipuriyum nōkkattuṭaṉ aruḷmiku vipulānanta aṭikaḷār avarkaḷ patiṉāṉkāṇṭukaḷ aritiṉ ārāyntu yāḻnūl eṉṉum icittamiḻārāycci nūliṉi iyaṟṟiṉārkaḷ. innūl carvacittu. vikāci 22 (5-6-1947) āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār tirunāḷilē cōḻanāṭṭut tirukkoḷḷam pūtūril muttamiḻ virakarākiya tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār muṉṉiliyiṟ kūṭiya pulavar pēraviyilē ciṟappāka araṅkēṟiyatu.
tirukkoḷḷam pūtūrt tiruppaṇic celvarum centamiḻp pēraṉparum ākiya naccāntuppaṭṭi uyar tiruvāḷar pe. rāma. rāma. citamparañ ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷatu poruḷutaviyuṭaṉ karantit tamiḻc caṅkattiṉarāl veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa yāḻnūl eṉṉum icittamiḻ ārāycci nūlāṉatu, tamiḻ makkaḷatu viḻippiṉmiyāl vaḻakkaṟṟu maṟinta paḻantamiḻ icimarapiṉi mīṭṭum uruvākki vaḷarkkum icit tamiḻk karuvūlamākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
cilappatikāram araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil yāḻāciriyaṉatu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟum irupattintaṭikaḷukku iyinta toru virivuriyāka eḻunta innūl, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappatikārattiṟ potintu vittuḷḷa ici nūṟporuḷit teḷivāka viḷakkukiṉṟatu. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḻakkoḻintupōṉa paḻantamiḻ iciyilakkaṇatti varalāṟṟu neṟiyil ārāyntu viḷakkum niliyil aminta innūl, paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻ muṉṉōr kaṇṭuṇarttiya nūṟṟu mūṉṟu tamiḻp paṇkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷiyum appaṇkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟku nilikkaḷamākiya ēḻperumpāli aiñciṟupāliyākiya paṉṉiru pālikaḷiṉ amippiṉiyum tantu avaṟṟi ikkālattil vīṇi mutaliya karuvikaḷil vittu vācittaṟku vēṇṭiya alaku nilikaḷiyum kuṟippiṭṭup paḻantamiḻici marapiṟkup puttuyiraḷikkiṉṟatu. tamiḻmuṉṉōr koṇṭa irupattiraṇṭu iciyalakukaḷ (curutikaḷ) iviyeṉat teḷivupaṭuttiyum irupattumūṉṟāvatāka niṉṟa piramāṇa curutiyeṉappaṭum vitiyiciyiṉ aḷaviṉiyum aḵtu aḷapiṟciṟitāyiṉum eṇṇikkiyil īralaku peṟuvatu eṉṉum nuṭpattiṉiyum naṉku viḷakkum muṟiyil yāḻ nūlāciriyar amittutaviya curuti vīṇi eṉṉum karuvi paḻantamiḻ iciyuruvaṅkaḷiyum vaṭanāṭṭilum teṉṉāṭ ṭṭilum piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya iciyurūvaṅkaḷiyum oruṅku vācittaṟkēṟṟa amippiṉiyuṭiyatākum.
ikkālattil karunāṭaka caṅkītameṉa vaḻaṅkum teṉṉāṭṭiciyil eḻupattiraṇṭu mēḷakarttā rākaṅkaḷi vakuttu muṟippaṭuttiyavar vēṅkaṭamakiyāvār. avar vakuttuḷḷa eḻupattiraṇṭu rākaṅkaḷik kuṟittu ici yāciriyarkaḷiṭiyē karuttu vēṟupāṭu nilaviyatu. inniliyil vēṅkaṭamaki eṉpār kiyāṇṭa vīṇik karuviyiṉ amippiṉi uḷḷavāṟu pulappaṭuttu tal ici vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyātatu ṉṉakkarutiya yāḻ nūlāciriyaravarkaḷ munnūṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḻuk kaṟṟukkiṭanta caturtaṇṭi vīṇiyi mīṭṭum uruvākki akkaruviyiṉ vāyilāka vēṅkaṭamaki kuṟitta eḻupattiraṇṭu irākaṅ kaḷiyum vīṇivittakar caṅkītāpūṣaṇam tiru ka. peṉya civāṉantam avarkaḷikkoṇṭu yārnūl araṅkēṟṟa viḻāvil vācittukkāṭṭac ceytārkaḷ vēṅkaṭamaki kiyāṇṭa caturtaṇṭi vīṇi eṉṉum ikkaruvi, piñca curuti riṣapattil cutta kāntākamum pañca curuti tivatattil cutta nitamum pēcuvatuṣa eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉi icip pulavar aṉivarum eḷitiṉ uṇarumpaṭi caturtaṇṭi vīṇiyiṉi uruvākkiyaḷitta perumi yāḻ nūlāciriyark kuriya taṉic ciṟappākum.
yāḻ nūlilaminta yāmuṟuppiyal eṉṟa pakuti, paṇṭittamiḻar vācitta icikkaruviyākiya yāḻiṉ vakikaḷiyum ataṉkaṇ aminta uṟuppukkaḷiyum ōviyamāka varintu kāṭṭi yāḻkkaruviyiṉi mīṭṭum uruvākki vācittuk kāṭṭutaṟkēṟṟa ceymuṟiyamip pākattikaḻkiṉṟatu. perūṅkalam eṉṉum. āyira narampu yāḻiṉ kaṇakkiṉik kaṇṭaṟintu ataṉi mīṭṭum amippataṟku vēṇṭiya kuṟippukkaḷ yāḻnūlil tarap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ici narampukaḷiṉ aciveṇ vikitam, narampu nīḷavikitam, katavilakkam mutaliya icikkaṇita muṟikaḷum kuḻal yāḻ mutaliya icikkaruvikaḷi ākkum muṟiyum yāḻnūlil teḷivupaṭuttappeṟṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu iciyārāyccikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappeṟum kaṇakku muṟikaḷiyum kuḻal yāḻ vīṇi mutaliya icikkaruvikaḷic ceytu payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷiyum viḷakkum yāḻnūlāṉatu, iciyilakkaṇa nūlāka maṭṭumaṉṟic ceymuṟi icittamiḻ nūlākavum amintatuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakuvatākum tamiḻici vaḷarcciyil ārvamuḷḷa iyalicip pulavarkaḷum māṇavarkaḷum yāḻ nūliṉi naṉkupayiṉṟu icittamiḻ vaḷampeṟa iṭi viṭātu uḻittalvēṇṭum. tamiḻici yil ārvamuḷḷa centamiḻc celvarkaḷum pēraṟiñarkaḷum paḻiya tamiḻicik karuviyākiya yāḻiṉi mīṭṭum uruvākkit takka icip pulavarkaḷi ātarittu akkaruvi yiṉi vācittum payiṟci peṟac ceytalum ivvāṟē maṟintupōṉa paḻantamiḻicik karuvikaḷi uruvākki vaḻakkattiṟkuk koṇarntu payiṟciyiṉiyaḷittut tamiḻiciyiṉ ciṟappiṉi ulaka makkaḷ uṇaram vaṇṇam ceytalum caṅka kālam mutal iṉṟaḷavum āyirakkaṇakkil iyaṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷa icittamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ tamiḻaka iciyaraṅkukaḷiṟ ciṟappākap pāṭappeṟumāṟu ūkka maḷittalum tamiḻicikku ākkamāṉa paṇikaḷākum. tamiḻmakkaḷ ticiyaṉittiṉ perumiyelān teṉṟiciyē veṉṟēṟavum acivil ceḻuntamiḻ vaḻakkē ayal vaḻakkiṉ tuṟi vellavum icimuḻutum meyyaṟivum iṭaṅkoḷḷum niliperukavum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷāl iyaṉṟapaṇikaḷi ārvamuṭaṉ puritalvēṇṭum eṉa aṉpuṭaṉ vēṇṭi innūliṉ niṟivu ceykiṉṟēṉ.

1.	3-76-4, 2. 1-66-10, 3. 3-76-4, 4. 1-24-7, 3-9-6. 4-21-5, 5-92-2. 7-84-5. 6-9-9, 6-21-1, 6-7.36-9. 7. 1-75-4, 4-66-5. 6-4-5, 6-10-2, 6-83-3, 7-36-9. 8.3 59-4, 3-85-7, 4-111-8.9, 5-7-1, 6-4-7, 6-13-8, 7-36-9. 9. 1-75-4, 4-111-8, 610-2, 6-97-8. 10. 3-81-2-7-36-9. 11. 1-66-10, 3-76-4, 4. 111-8, 7-36-9 12. 3-57-5, 4-111-3, 7-36-9 13. 2-94-6, 7-36-9 14. 1-73-6, 1-87-8, 2. 57-3, 3-103-6, 6-49-6. 15. 6-10-2. 16. 1-6-5, 1-73-8, 2-6-1, 3 8-4, 4-6-6, 6-36-8, 6-77-1. 17. 1-44-5, 2-12-3, 3-8-4, 3-85-5, 4-111-8, 6-10-2, 6-40-2.
